FILE: ./tests/integration/test_persistence.py
======================================================================
# Copyright 2026 Lightibulb

"""Integration tests for the SQLite persistence layer."""

import datetime as dt
import sqlite3

import pytest

from ecofac.domain.models import EnergyReading
from ecofac.persistence.database import get_connection, init_db
from ecofac.persistence.repository import DuplicateReadingError, insert_reading


@pytest.fixture
def db_conn():
    """Yield an in-memory database connection initialized with the schema."""
    conn = get_connection(":memory:")
    init_db(conn)
    yield conn
    conn.close()


def test_schema_initialization(db_conn):
    """Verify tables are created."""
    cursor = db_conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';")
    tables = {row["name"] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
    assert "organizations" in tables
    assert "energy_readings" in tables
    assert "audit_events" in tables


def test_identifier_uniqueness(db_conn):
    """Verify primary keys prevent duplicate organizations."""
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat()

    db_conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        ("org-1", "Test", now)
    )

    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        db_conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
            ("org-1", "Duplicate", now)
        )


def test_relationships(db_conn):
    """Verify foreign keys restrict invalid associations."""
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat()
    # Attempting to add a building to a non-existent org should fail
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        db_conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
            ("b-1", "org-999", "Test Bldg", now)
        )


def test_duplicate_reading_handling(db_conn):
    """Verify duplicate readings raise an error and do not silently overwrite."""
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat()

    db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES ('o1', 'O1', ?)", (now,))
    db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('b1', 'o1', 'B1', ?)", (now,))
    db_conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO meters (id, building_id, fuel_type, unit, created_at) "
        "VALUES ('m1', 'b1', 'electricity', 'kWh', ?)",
        (now,)
    )
    db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, created_at) VALUES ('s1', 'S1', 'csv', ?)", (now,))
    db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO import_batches (id, source_id, status, imported_at) VALUES ('ib1', 's1', 'complete', ?)", (now,))

    r1 = EnergyReading(
        id="r1", meter_id="m1", timestamp_utc="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
        value=10.5, unit="kWh", source_id="s1", batch_id="ib1",
        is_valid=True, created_at=now
    )
    insert_reading(db_conn, r1)

    # Attempt duplicate
    r2 = EnergyReading(
        id="r2", meter_id="m1", timestamp_utc="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
        value=20.0, unit="kWh", source_id="s1", batch_id="ib1",
        is_valid=True, created_at=now
    )
    with pytest.raises(DuplicateReadingError):
        insert_reading(db_conn, r2)

    # Verify value was not overwritten
    cursor = db_conn.execute("SELECT value FROM energy_readings WHERE meter_id='m1'")
    assert cursor.fetchone()["value"] == 10.5


def test_transaction_rollback(db_conn):
    """Verify that a failed insert rolls back completely."""
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat()

    db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES ('o2', 'O2', ?)", (now,))

    try:
        db_conn.execute("BEGIN")
        db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('b2', 'o2', 'B2', ?)", (now,))
        # Force an error by inserting a duplicate primary key
        db_conn.execute("INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('b2', 'o2', 'B2-dupe', ?)", (now,))
        db_conn.execute("COMMIT")
    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        db_conn.execute("ROLLBACK")

    # Verify the first insert was rolled back
    cursor = db_conn.execute("SELECT count(*) as c FROM buildings WHERE id='b2'")
    assert cursor.fetchone()["c"] == 0


def test_persistence_across_restarts(tmp_path):
    """Verify data persists when closing and reopening the connection."""
    db_file = tmp_path / "test.db"

    # 1. Write data
    conn1 = get_connection(str(db_file))
    init_db(conn1)
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat()
    conn1.execute("INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES ('o3', 'Persist', ?)", (now,))
    conn1.close()

    # 2. Read data after restart
    conn2 = get_connection(str(db_file))
    cursor = conn2.execute("SELECT name FROM organizations WHERE id='o3'")
    assert cursor.fetchone()["name"] == "Persist"
    conn2.close()

# Copyright 2026 Lightibulb
"""Integration tests for the data quality engine."""
import datetime as dt

import pytest

from ecofac.domain.models import EnergyReading
from ecofac.persistence.database import get_connection, init_db
from ecofac.persistence.repository import insert_reading
from ecofac.quality.assessment import assess_quality


@pytest.fixture
def db_conn():
    """Yield an in-memory database seeded with prerequisite data."""
    conn = get_connection(":memory:")
    init_db(conn)
    now = "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES ('o1', 'Org', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('b1', 'o1', 'Bldg', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO meters (id, building_id, fuel_type, unit, created_at) "
        "VALUES ('m1', 'b1', 'electricity', 'kWh', ?)",
        (now,),
    )
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, created_at) VALUES ('s1', 'Src', 'csv', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO import_batches (id, source_id, status, imported_at) VALUES ('ib1', 's1', 'started', ?)", (now,))
    yield conn
    conn.close()


def _insert_reading(conn, reading_id, ts, value, *, is_valid=True):
    r = EnergyReading(
        id=reading_id, meter_id="m1", timestamp_utc=ts,
        value=value, unit="kWh", source_id="s1", batch_id="ib1",
        is_valid=is_valid, created_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    )
    insert_reading(conn, r)


def test_perfect_data_eligibility(db_conn):
    """Verify perfect data is marked eligible and no flags are raised."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 10.0)
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r2", "2026-01-01T01:00:00Z", 12.0)
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r3", "2026-01-01T02:00:00Z", 14.0)
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 2, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.expected_readings == 3
    assert report.actual_readings == 3
    assert report.missing_readings == 0
    assert report.is_stale is False
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is True
    assert len(report.flags) == 0


def test_missing_data_detection(db_conn):
    """Verify missing readings lower eligibility."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 10.0)
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r3", "2026-01-01T02:00:00Z", 14.0)
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 2, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.missing_readings == 1
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is False
    assert "missing_data" in report.flags


def test_stale_data_detection(db_conn):
    """Verify old data is marked as stale."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 10.0)
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.is_stale is True
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is False
    assert "stale_data" in report.flags


def test_invalid_vs_extreme_valid_data(db_conn):
    """Verify extreme but valid data is kept, while invalid data fails eligibility."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 99999.9, is_valid=True)
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r2", "2026-01-01T01:00:00Z", -10.0, is_valid=False)
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r3", "2026-01-01T02:00:00Z", 14.0, is_valid=True)
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 2, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.invalid_readings == 1
    assert report.actual_readings == 3
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is False
    assert "invalid_data" in report.flags


def test_duplicate_detection(db_conn):
    """Verify duplicates logged during ingestion affect eligibility."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 10.0)
    db_conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO data_quality_issues "
        "(id, reading_id, batch_id, issue_type, description, meter_id, "
        "reading_timestamp_utc, created_at) "
        "VALUES ('dq1', NULL, 'ib1', 'duplicate', "
        "'Duplicate in CSV: m1 @ 2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', 'm1', "
        "'2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z')"
    )
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.duplicate_readings == 1
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is False
    assert "duplicate_data" in report.flags


def test_duplicate_outside_window_is_ignored(db_conn):
    """Duplicates outside the assessed period must not poison eligibility."""
    _insert_reading(db_conn, "r1", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", 10.0)
    db_conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO data_quality_issues "
        "(id, reading_id, batch_id, issue_type, description, meter_id, "
        "reading_timestamp_utc, created_at) "
        "VALUES ('dq2', NULL, 'ib1', 'duplicate', "
        "'Duplicate in CSV: m1 @ 2026-01-05T00:00:00Z', 'm1', "
        "'2026-01-05T00:00:00Z', '2026-01-05T00:00:00Z')"
    )
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=dt.UTC)
    report = assess_quality(db_conn, "m1", start, end, expected_frequency_minutes=60)
    assert report.duplicate_readings == 0
    assert report.is_analysis_eligible is True

FILE: ./tests/integration/test_regressions.py

# Copyright 2026 Lightibulb
"""Regression tests for ingestion and time handling fixes."""
import pytest

from ecofac.ingestion.pipeline import ingest_csv
from ecofac.persistence.database import get_connection, init_db


@pytest.fixture
def db_conn():
    """Yield an in-memory database seeded with prerequisite data."""
    conn = get_connection(":memory:")
    init_db(conn)
    now = "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO organizations (id, name, created_at) VALUES ('o1', 'Org', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO buildings (id, org_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('b1', 'o1', 'Bldg', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO meters (id, building_id, fuel_type, unit, created_at) "
        "VALUES ('m1', 'b1', 'electricity', 'kWh', ?)",
        (now,),
    )
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, created_at) VALUES ('s1', 'Src', 'csv', ?)", (now,))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO import_batches (id, source_id, status, imported_at) VALUES ('ib1', 's1', 'started', ?)", (now,))
    yield conn
    conn.close()


def test_naive_timestamp_is_rejected(db_conn):
    """Naive timestamps must be rejected, not interpreted as local time."""
    csv_data = (
        "timestamp,building_id,meter_id,energy_value,unit\n"
        "2026-01-01T12:00:00,b1,m1,10.5,kWh\n"
    )
    summary = ingest_csv(db_conn, csv_data, "s1", "ib1")
    assert summary.total_rows == 1
    assert summary.accepted_rows == 0
    assert summary.rejected_rows == 1
    assert summary.invalid_timestamps == 1


def test_aware_offset_timestamp_still_normalized(db_conn):
    """Timezone-aware offsets remain normalized to UTC 'Z' storage form."""
    csv_data = (
        "timestamp,building_id,meter_id,energy_value,unit\n"
        "2026-01-01T12:00:00+02:00,b1,m1,10.5,kWh\n"
    )
    summary = ingest_csv(db_conn, csv_data, "s1", "ib1")
    assert summary.accepted_rows == 1
    row = db_conn.execute("SELECT timestamp_utc FROM energy_readings").fetchone()
    assert row["timestamp_utc"] == "2026-01-01T10:00:00Z"


def test_duplicate_issue_is_scoped_with_typed_columns(db_conn):
    """Logged duplicate issues carry meter_id and reading timestamp."""
    csv_data = (
        "timestamp,building_id,meter_id,energy_value,unit\n"
        "2026-01-01T12:00:00Z,b1,m1,10.5,kWh\n"
        "2026-01-01T12:00:00Z,b1,m1,20.0,kWh\n"
    )
    ingest_csv(db_conn, csv_data, "s1", "ib1")
    row = db_conn.execute(
        "SELECT meter_id, reading_timestamp_utc FROM data_quality_issues "
        "WHERE issue_type = 'duplicate'"
    ).fetchone()
    assert row["meter_id"] == "m1"
    assert row["reading_timestamp_utc"] == "2026-01-01T12:00:00Z"

# Copyright 2026 Lightibulb
"""Minimal health-check tests for CI bootstrapping."""

from ecofac import __version__


def test_package_imports() -> None:
    """The package must import and expose a non-empty version."""
    assert isinstance(__version__, str)
    assert __version__ != ""


def test_version_is_dotted_numeric() -> None:
    """Version must be at least two dotted numeric parts."""
    parts = __version__.split(".")
    assert len(parts) >= 2
    assert all(part.isdigit() for part in parts)
